All core enforcement tasks passed. Several environment-specific issues were encountered and resolved — documented below so production deployment goes smoothly.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eTest Approach\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-test-approach\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Test Approach\" href=\"#test-approach\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe hardening environment used for enforcement testing did not have AI Units billing data, so the DQL task was patched to inject a test user directly and the lockout JavaScript was modified to hardcode an email address. This allowed full validation of the enforcement chain. Real billing event schema was subsequently validated against an internal dev tenant — see §Billing Event Schema Validation below.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTemporary changes made for testing (do not carry into production):\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTask\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eTemporary change\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003echeck_for_ai_quota\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReplaced real DQL with \u003ccode\u003efilter false\u003c/code\u003e + hardcoded test email inject\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003elock_out_users\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eReplaced credential vault lookup with hardcoded \u003ccode\u003eaccountId\u003c/code\u003e and OAuth secret\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003elock_out_users\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eRemoved predecessor condition to allow unconditional execution\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAll changes were reverted before committing the production \u003ccode\u003eworkflow.yaml\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e1. DQL does not support block comments (\u003ccode\u003e/* ... */\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-1-dql-does-not-support-block-comments---\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 1. DQL does not support block comments (/* ... */)\" href=\"#1-dql-does-not-support-block-comments---\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSymptom:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003echeck_for_ai_quota\u003c/code\u003e failed with \u003ccode\u003ePARSE_ERROR: ':' isn't allowed here\u003c/code\u003e.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eCause:\u003c/strong\u003e The DQL query contained a \u003ccode\u003e/* ... */\u003c/code\u003e block comment, which the Grail parser does not support.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eFix:\u003c/strong\u003e Removed all block comments from the DQL. Single-line \u003ccode\u003e//\u003c/code\u003e comments within pipeline steps are supported.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eProduction impact:\u003c/strong\u003e The production \u003ccode\u003eworkflow.yaml\u003c/code\u003e in this repo has no block comments — this is already fixed.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2. Hardening environment requires different OAuth and API base URLs\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-2-hardening-environment-requires-different-oauth-and-api-base-urls\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2. Hardening environment requires different OAuth and API base URLs\" href=\"#2-hardening-environment-requires-different-oauth-and-api-base-urls\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSymptom:\u003c/strong\u003e OAuth token request returned \u003ccode\u003e400 invalid_request\u003c/code\u003e with empty error description.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eCause:\u003c/strong\u003e The workflow uses \u003ccode\u003esso.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eapi.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e (production SaaS defaults), which are unreachable from the hardening environment.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eFix:\u003c/strong\u003e Swapped \u003ccode\u003etokenUrl\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003ebaseUrl\u003c/code\u003e to the internal environment equivalents of the SSO and Account Management API endpoints.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eProduction impact:\u003c/strong\u003e None. The production \u003ccode\u003eworkflow.yaml\u003c/code\u003e uses \u003ccode\u003esso.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003eapi.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e, which are correct for customer SaaS environments.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. Outbound hosts must be in the AppEngine allowlist\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-3-outbound-hosts-must-be-in-the-appengine-allowlist\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3. Outbound hosts must be in the AppEngine allowlist\" href=\"#3-outbound-hosts-must-be-in-the-appengine-allowlist\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSymptom:\u003c/strong\u003e After correcting the URLs, requests were blocked with \u003ccode\u003eNotCapable: Blocked request to '...' (host not in allowlist)\u003c/code\u003e.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eCause:\u003c/strong\u003e AppEngine sandboxes outbound connections to an explicit allowlist.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eFix:\u003c/strong\u003e The hardening environment already had the sprint/hardening SSO and API hosts in its allowlist. No action was needed beyond selecting the correct URLs.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eProduction impact:\u003c/strong\u003e For production customer environments, \u003ccode\u003esso.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eapi.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e should already be in the default allowlist. If a customer hits this error, they need to add those hosts via \u003cstrong\u003eSettings → Preferences → Allowlist for outbound connections\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4. Credential vault \u003ccode\u003egetCredentialsDetails\u003c/code\u003e returned 403 in hardening\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-4-credential-vault-getcredentialsdetails-returned-403-in-hardening\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 4. Credential vault getCredentialsDetails returned 403 in hardening\" href=\"#4-credential-vault-getcredentialsdetails-returned-403-in-hardening\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSymptom:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003ecredentialVaultClient.getCredentialsDetails\u003c/code\u003e returned \u003ccode\u003e403: The requested credential details cannot be accessed by the current user or entity\u003c/code\u003e even with correct AppEngine scope and access settings.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eCause:\u003c/strong\u003e Not fully diagnosed — likely a hardening-environment-specific restriction on credential access for AppEngine workflows. The actor authorization settings, credential scope, and access were all correctly configured.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eWorkaround:\u003c/strong\u003e Credentials were hardcoded directly in the script for testing purposes and removed before committing.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eProduction impact:\u003c/strong\u003e This 403 was not reproduced in any production SaaS environment. The production \u003ccode\u003eworkflow.yaml\u003c/code\u003e uses the standard \u003ccode\u003egetCredentialsDetails\u003c/code\u003e approach (identical to the original \u003ca href=\"https://github.com/Dynatrace/community-examples/tree/main/cost-intelligence-blueprints/set-quotas-with-workflow\"\u003eLog Query Quota Workflow\u003c/a\u003e which works in production). Monitor this on first production deployment and escalate if the 403 reappears.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e5. Workflow actor authorization settings required\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-5-workflow-actor-authorization-settings-required\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 5. Workflow actor authorization settings required\" href=\"#5-workflow-actor-authorization-settings-required\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSymptom:\u003c/strong\u003e First execution failed with \u003ccode\u003eCould not run workflow task on behalf of '\u0026lt;uuid\u0026gt;'. Please ensure Authorization Settings are configured\u003c/code\u003e.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eCause:\u003c/strong\u003e The workflow Actor (service user) had not been configured in \u003cstrong\u003eSettings → Automations → Authorization settings\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eFix:\u003c/strong\u003e Changed the Actor to the deploying user, who already had full authorization settings configured.\u003cbr\u003e\n\u003cstrong\u003eProduction impact:\u003c/strong\u003e Documented in the README setup steps. The Actor must have Authorization Settings configured before the workflow can execute.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" publishing:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul class=\"contains-task-list\"\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e \u003ccode\u003eevent.type == \"AI Units\"\u003c/code\u003e confirmed against production billing events (\u003ccode\u003efetch dt.system.events | filter event.kind == \"BILLING_USAGE_EVENT\" | dedup event.type\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e Metric field \u003ccode\u003eusage.quantity.billable\u003c/code\u003e confirmed against a real AI Units billing event (see §Billing Event Schema Validation below)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e Quota threshold (default: 1,000) reviewed and adjusted for the target customer\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e IAM deny policy created and attached to \"AI Quota Exceeded\" group (consult Dynatrace IAM docs for the appropriate permission to deny)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e OAuth client created with an Account Manager user as subject, scopes: \u003ccode\u003eaccount-idm-read\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003eaccount-idm-write\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e Credential vault entry \u003ccode\u003eQuota check OAuth\u003c/code\u003e created (Username = Account UUID, Password = full OAuth client secret, Scope = AppEngine)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e Workflow Actor has Authorization Settings configured\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e \u003ccode\u003esso.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eapi.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e are in the environment's outbound allowlist\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e \u003ccode\u003esend_email_about_ai_quota\u003c/code\u003e tested with a real email recipient before go-live\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli class=\"task-list-item\"\u003e\u003cinput type=\"checkbox\" id=\"\" disabled=\"\" class=\"task-list-item-checkbox\" aria-label=\"Incomplete task\"\u003e \u003ccode\u003equota_reset_at_midnight\u003c/code\u003e validated after first production run at 00:00 UTC\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBilling Event Schema aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eA real \u003ccode\u003eAI Units\u003c/code\u003e billing event was observed in an internal Dynatrace environment on 2026-09-23. The event confirmed the following field schema:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cmarkdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\u003ctable\u003e\n\u003cthead\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eField\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eValue observed\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003cth\u003eWorkflow uses\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eevent.kind\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eBILLING_USAGE_EVENT\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efilter event.kind == \"BILLING_USAGE_EVENT\"\u003c/code\u003e ✓\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eevent.type\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eAI Units\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003efilter event.type == \"AI Units\"\u003c/code\u003e ✓\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eusage.quantity.billable\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e30.00\u003c/code\u003e (double)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003esum(usage.quantity.billable)\u003c/code\u003e ✓\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eusage.unit\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eUnits\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e— (informational)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003euser.email\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003epresent (e.g. \u003ccode\u003euser@dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eby:{user.email}\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003efields email = user.email\u003c/code\u003e ✓\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eevent.id\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eUUID\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003ededup event.id\u003c/code\u003e ✓\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eevent.version\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e1.0.0\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003cem\u003e(filter removed — correct, \"1.0.0\" ≠ \"1.0\")\u003c/em\u003e ✓\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eevent.provider\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003eLIMA_USAGE_STREAM\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e— (not filtered)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003edt.openpipeline.source\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003esystem_events\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003efetched via \u003ccode\u003efetch dt.system.events\u003c/code\u003e ✓\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKey findings:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eusage.quantity.billable\u003c/code\u003e is confirmed as the correct metric field. The field \u003ccode\u003econsumed_units\u003c/code\u003e does not exist in real events.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eevent.version\u003c/code\u003e is \u003ccode\u003e\"1.0.0\"\u003c/code\u003e, not \u003ccode\u003e\"1.0\"\u003c/code\u003e. Removing the \u003ccode\u003eevent.version == \"1.0\"\u003c/code\u003e filter (present in the original log quota blueprint) was correct — keeping it would have caused the query to silently return no results.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003euser.email\u003c/code\u003e is always present (1,463/1,463 events checked had a non-null value). Per-user quota enforcement is safe to rely on.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eusage.start\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003eusage.end\u003c/code\u003e are both null in practice. The event \u003ccode\u003etimestamp\u003c/code\u003e is the usage time, confirming \u003ccode\u003efrom: -0d@d\u003c/code\u003e as the correct daily window. \u003cstrong\u003eReviewer note:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003e-0d@d\u003c/code\u003e is DQL's calendar-day anchor — the \u003ccode\u003e@d\u003c/code\u003e suffix snaps to midnight UTC at the start of today, making it a calendar-day window (00:00 UTC → now), not a rolling \u003ccode\u003enow()-24h\u003c/code\u003e. This is intentional and aligns with the midnight reset in \u003ccode\u003equota_reset_at_midnight\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecaller.type\u003c/code\u003e takes three observed values: \u003ccode\u003einternal\u003c/code\u003e (workflow/operator actions — the dominant type), \u003ccode\u003eapi\u003c/code\u003e (direct API calls), and \u003ccode\u003emcp\u003c/code\u003e (MCP tool calls). All share \u003ccode\u003etool.category: ai\u003c/code\u003e. The workflow counts all caller types toward the quota. Customers who want to restrict the quota to specific interaction types (e.g. only \u003ccode\u003eapi\u003c/code\u003e calls) can add \u003ccode\u003e| filter caller.type == \"api\"\u003c/code\u003e to both DQL tasks.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eService accounts appear with a UUID-based email format (\u003ccode\u003e\u0026lt;uuid\u0026gt;@service.sso.dynatrace.com\u003c/code\u003e). The quota applies to these identities as well. Customers who want to exempt service accounts can add \u003ccode\u003e| filterOut matchesPhrase(user.email, \"@service.sso.dynatrace.com\")\u003c/code\u003e to both DQL tasks.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eReal-world consumption over 7 days in an active internal tenant ranged from ~51 to ~38,250 units per user. Only one user exceeded 1,000 units in a single day, validating that 1,000 units/day is a reasonable default threshold for most customer environments..\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/article\u003e","richTextTruncated":false,"renderedFileInfo":null,"symbols":{"timed_out":false,"not_analyzed":false,"symbols":[{"name":"Testing Testing \u0026 Validation Report","","**Workflow:** AI Units Quota Check  ","**Test date:** 2026-09-23  ","**Environment:** Internal sprint/hardening environment (non-production SaaS)","","---","","## Summary","","End-to-end validation of the workflow was completed successfully in a hardening environment. All core enforcement tasks passed. Several environment-specific issues were encountered and resolved — documented below so production deployment goes smoothly.","","---","","## Test Approach","","The hardening environment used for enforcement testing did not have AI Units billing data, so the DQL task was patched to inject a test user directly and the lockout JavaScript was modified to hardcode an email address. This allowed full validation of the enforcement chain. Real billing event schema was subsequently validated against an internal dev tenant — see §Billing Event Schema Validation below.","","**Temporary changes made for testing (do not carry into production):**","","| Task | Temporary change |","|---|---|","| `check_for_ai_quota` | Replaced real DQL with `filter false` + hardcoded test email inject |","| `lock_out_users` | Replaced credential vault lookup with hardcoded `accountId` and OAuth secret |","| `lock_out_users` | Removed predecessor condition to allow unconditional execution |","","All changes were reverted before committing the production `workflow.yaml`.","","---","","## Test Results","","| Check | Result | Notes |","|---|---|---|","| `check_for_ai_quota` runs successfully with empty result | PASS | \"Fail on empty result\" must be OFF |","| `lock_out_users` obtains OAuth token | PASS | See hardening-specific URL note below |","| `lock_out_users` finds \"AI Quota Exceeded\" group via Account Management API | PASS | |","| Test user added to \"AI Quota Exceeded\" group | PASS | Verified in Account Management UI |","| Business event `ai.units.quota.exceeded` ingested | PASS | 3 records visible in `fetch bizevents` |","| `quota_reset_at_midnight` logic | NOT TESTED | Condition requires 00:00 UTC; validated by code review only |","| `send_email_about_ai_quota` delivery | NOT TESTED | Skipped (task was discarded due to removed condition during testing) |","| De-duplication via bizevents filterOut | NOT TESTED | Requires real billing events; validated by code review only |","","---","","## Issues Encountered \u0026 Resolutions","","### 1. DQL does not support block comments (`/* ... */`)","","**Symptom:** `check_for_ai_quota` failed with `PARSE_ERROR: ':' isn't allowed here`.  ","**Cause:** The DQL query contained a `/* ... */` block comment, which the Grail parser does not support.  ","**Fix:** Removed all block comments from the DQL. Single-line `//` comments within pipeline steps are supported.  ","**Production impact:** The production `workflow.yaml` in this repo has no block comments — this is already fixed.","","---","","### 2. Hardening environment requires different OAuth and API base URLs","","**Symptom:** OAuth token request returned `400 invalid_request` with empty error description.  ","**Cause:** The workflow uses `sso.dynatrace.com` and `api.dynatrace.com` (production SaaS defaults), which are unreachable from the hardening environment.  ","**Fix:** Swapped `tokenUrl` and `baseUrl` to the internal environment equivalents of the SSO and Account Management API endpoints.  ","**Production impact:** None. The production `workflow.yaml` uses `sso.dynatrace.com` / `api.dynatrace.com`, which are correct for customer SaaS environments.","","---","","### 3. Outbound hosts must be in the AppEngine allowlist","","**Symptom:** After correcting the URLs, requests were blocked with `NotCapable: Blocked request to '...' (host not in allowlist)`.  ","**Cause:** AppEngine sandboxes outbound connections to an explicit allowlist.  ","**Fix:** The hardening environment already had the sprint/hardening SSO and API hosts in its allowlist. No action was needed beyond selecting the correct URLs.  ","**Production impact:** For production customer environments, `sso.dynatrace.com` and `api.dynatrace.com` should already be in the default allowlist. If a customer hits this error, they need to add those hosts via **Settings → Preferences → Allowlist for outbound connections**.","","---","","### 4. Credential vault `getCredentialsDetails` returned 403 in hardening","","**Symptom:** `credentialVaultClient.getCredentialsDetails` returned `403: The requested credential details cannot be accessed by the current user or entity` even with correct AppEngine scope and access settings.  ","**Cause:** Not fully diagnosed — likely a hardening-environment-specific restriction on credential access for AppEngine workflows. The actor authorization settings, credential scope, and access were all correctly configured.  ","**Workaround:** Credentials were hardcoded directly in the script for testing purposes and removed before committing.  ","**Production impact:** This 403 was not reproduced in any production SaaS environment. The production `workflow.yaml` uses the standard `getCredentialsDetails` approach (identical to the original [Log Query Quota Workflow](https://github.com/Dynatrace/community-examples/tree/main/cost-intelligence-blueprints/set-quotas-with-workflow) which works in production). Monitor this on first production deployment and escalate if the 403 reappears.","","---","","### 5. Workflow actor authorization settings required","","**Symptom:** First execution failed with `Could not run workflow task on behalf of '\u003cuuid\u003e'. Please ensure Authorization Settings are configured`.  ","**Cause:** The workflow Actor (service user) had not been configured in **Settings → Automations → Authorization settings**.  ","**Fix:** Changed the Actor to the deploying user, who already had full authorization settings configured.  ","**Production impact:** Documented in the README setup steps. The Actor must have Authorization Settings configured before the workflow can execute.","","---","","## Production Deployment Checklist","","For the colleague deploying to production, verify the following before publishing:","","- [ ] `event.type == \"AI Units\"` confirmed against production billing events (`fetch dt.system.events | filter event.kind == \"BILLING_USAGE_EVENT\" | dedup event.type`)","- [ ] Metric field `usage.quantity.billable` confirmed against a real AI Units billing event (see §Billing Event Schema Validation below)","- [ ] Quota threshold (default: 1,000) reviewed and adjusted for the target customer","- [ ] IAM deny policy created and attached to \"AI Quota Exceeded\" group (consult Dynatrace IAM docs for the appropriate permission to deny)","- [ ] OAuth client created with an Account Manager user as subject, scopes: `account-idm-read`, `account-idm-write`","- [ ] Credential vault entry `Quota check OAuth` created (Username = Account UUID, Password = full OAuth client secret, Scope = AppEngine)","- [ ] Workflow Actor has Authorization Settings configured","- [ ] `sso.dynatrace.com` and `api.dynatrace.com` are in the environment's outbound allowlist","- [ ] `send_email_about_ai_quota` tested with a real email recipient before go-live","- [ ] `quota_reset_at_midnight` validated after first production run at 00:00 UTC","","---","","## Billing Event Schema Validation","","A real `AI Units` billing event was observed in an internal Dynatrace environment on 2026-09-23. The event confirmed the following field schema:","","| Field | Value observed | Workflow uses |","|---|---|---|","| `event.kind` | `BILLING_USAGE_EVENT` | `filter event.kind == \"BILLING_USAGE_EVENT\"` ✓ |","| `event.type` | `AI Units` | `filter event.type == \"AI Units\"` ✓ |","| `usage.quantity.billable` | `30.00` (double) | `sum(usage.quantity.billable)` ✓ |","| `usage.unit` | `Units` | — (informational) |","| `user.email` | present (e.g. `user@dynatrace.com`) | `by:{user.email}` / `fields email = user.email` ✓ |","| `event.id` | UUID | `dedup event.id` ✓ |","| `event.version` | `1.0.0` | *(filter removed — correct, \"1.0.0\" ≠ \"1.0\")* ✓ |","| `event.provider` | `LIMA_USAGE_STREAM` | — (not filtered) |","| `dt.openpipeline.source` | `system_events` | fetched via `fetch dt.system.events` ✓ |","","**Key findings:**","","- `usage.quantity.billable` is confirmed as the correct metric field. The field `consumed_units` does not exist in real events.","- `event.version` is `\"1.0.0\"`, not `\"1.0\"`. Removing the `event.version == \"1.0\"` filter (present in the original log quota blueprint) was correct — keeping it would have caused the query to silently return no results.","- `user.email` is always present (1,463/1,463 events checked had a non-null value). Per-user quota enforcement is safe to rely on.","- `usage.start` and `usage.end` are both null in practice. The event `timestamp` is the usage time, confirming `from: -0d@d` as the correct daily window. **Reviewer note:** `-0d@d` is DQL's calendar-day anchor — the `@d` suffix snaps to midnight UTC at the start of today, making it a calendar-day window (00:00 UTC → now), not a rolling `now()-24h`. This is intentional and aligns with the midnight reset in `quota_reset_at_midnight`.","- `caller.type` takes three observed values: `internal` (workflow/operator actions — the dominant type), `api` (direct API calls), and `mcp` (MCP tool calls). All share `tool.category: ai`. The workflow counts all caller types toward the quota. Customers who want to restrict the quota to specific interaction types (e.g. only `api` calls) can add `| filter caller.type == \"api\"` to both DQL tasks.","- Service accounts appear with a UUID-based email format (`\u003cuuid\u003e@service.sso.dynatrace.com`). The quota applies to these identities as well. Customers who want to exempt service accounts can add `| filterOut matchesPhrase(user.email, \"@service.sso.dynatrace.com\")` to both DQL tasks.","- Real-world consumption over 7 days in an active internal tenant ranged from ~51 to ~38,250 units per user. Only one user exceeded 1,000 units in a single day, validating that 1,000 units/day is a reasonable default threshold for most customer environments.."],"stylingDirectives":null,"colorizedLines":["\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e# \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eTesting \u0026amp; Validation Report\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eWorkflow:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e AI Units Quota Check  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eTest date:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e 2026-09-23  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eEnvironment:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e Internal sprint/hardening environment (non-production SaaS)","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ms\"\u003e---\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e## \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003eSummary\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","End-to-end validation of the workflow was completed successfully in a hardening environment. ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eCause:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e The DQL query contained a \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e/* ... */\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e block comment, which the Grail parser does not support.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eFix:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e Removed all block comments from the DQL. Single-line \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e//\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e comments within pipeline steps are supported.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eProduction impact:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e The production \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eworkflow.yaml\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e in this repo has no block comments — this is already fixed.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ms\"\u003e---\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003e2. Hardening environment requires different OAuth and API base URLs\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eSymptom:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e OAuth token request returned \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e400 invalid_request\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e with empty error description.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eCause:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e The workflow uses \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esso.dynatrace.com\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e and \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eapi.dynatrace.com\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e (production SaaS defaults), which are unreachable from the hardening environment.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eFix:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e Swapped \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etokenUrl\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e and \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ebaseUrl\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e to the internal environment equivalents of the SSO and Account Management API endpoints.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eProduction impact:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e None. The production \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eworkflow.yaml\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e uses \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esso.dynatrace.com\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e / \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eapi.dynatrace.com\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e, which are correct for customer SaaS environments.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ms\"\u003e---\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003e3. Outbound hosts must be in the AppEngine allowlist\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eSymptom:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e After correcting the URLs, requests were blocked with \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eNotCapable: Blocked request to \u0026#39;...\u0026#39; (host not in allowlist)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eCause:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e AppEngine sandboxes outbound connections to an explicit allowlist.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eFix:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e The hardening environment already had the sprint/hardening SSO and API hosts in its allowlist. No action was needed beyond selecting the correct URLs.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eProduction impact:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e For production customer environments, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003esso.dynatrace.com\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e and \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eapi.dynatrace.com\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e should already be in the default allowlist. If a customer hits this error, they need to add those hosts via \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eSettings → Preferences → Allowlist for outbound connections\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ms\"\u003e---\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003e4. Credential vault \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003egetCredentialsDetails\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e returned 403 in hardening\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eSymptom:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecredentialVaultClient.getCredentialsDetails\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e returned \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e403: The requested credential details cannot be accessed by the current user or entity\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e even with correct AppEngine scope and access settings.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eCause:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e Not fully diagnosed — likely a hardening-environment-specific restriction on credential access for AppEngine workflows. The actor authorization settings, credential scope, and access were all correctly configured.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eWorkaround:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e Credentials were hardcoded directly in the script for testing purposes and removed before committing.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eProduction impact:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e This 403 was not reproduced in any production SaaS environment. The production \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eworkflow.yaml\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e uses the standard \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003egetCredentialsDetails\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e approach (identical to the original \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e[\u003c/span\u003eLog Query Quota Workflow\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e]\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e(\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-corl\"\u003ehttps://github.com/Dynatrace/community-examples/tree/main/cost-intelligence-blueprints/set-quotas-with-workflow\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e)\u003c/span\u003e which works in production). Monitor this on first production deployment and escalate if the 403 reappears.","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-ms\"\u003e---\u003c/span\u003e","","\u003cspan class=\"pl-mh\"\u003e### \u003cspan class=\"pl-en\"\u003e5. ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eCause:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e The workflow Actor (service user) had not been configured in \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eSettings → Automations → Authorization settings\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eFix:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e Changed the Actor to the deploying user, who already had full authorization settings configured.  ","\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eProduction impact:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e Documented in the README setup steps. Per-user quota enforcement is safe to rely on.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eusage.start\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e and \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eusage.end\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e are both null in practice. The event \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etimestamp\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e is the usage time, confirming \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efrom: -0d@d\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e as the correct daily window. \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003eReviewer note:\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e-0d@d\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e is DQL\u0026#39;s calendar-day anchor — the \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e@d\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e suffix snaps to midnight UTC at the start of today, making it a calendar-day window (00:00 UTC → now), not a rolling \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003enow()-24h\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e. This is intentional and aligns with the midnight reset in \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003equota_reset_at_midnight\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecaller.type\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e takes three observed values: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003einternal\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e (workflow/operator actions — the dominant type), \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eapi\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e (direct API calls), and \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003emcp\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e (MCP tool calls). All share \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003etool.category: ai\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e. The workflow counts all caller types toward the quota. Customers who want to restrict the quota to specific interaction types (e.g. only \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eapi\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e calls) can add \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e| filter caller.type == \u0026quot;api\u0026quot;\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e to both DQL tasks.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Service accounts appear with a UUID-based email format (\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e\u0026lt;uuid\u0026gt;@service.sso.dynatrace.com\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e). The quota applies to these identities as well. Customers who want to exempt service accounts can add \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e| filterOut matchesPhrase(user.email, \u0026quot;@service.sso.dynatrace.com\u0026quot;)\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e`\u003c/span\u003e to both DQL tasks.","\u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003e-\u003c/span\u003e Real-world consumption over 7 days in an active internal tenant ranged from \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e51 to \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e~\u003c/span\u003e38,250 units per user. Only one user exceeded 1,000 units in a single day, validating that 1,000 units/day is a reasonable default threshold for most customer environments.."]}},"title":"ai-units-quota-workflow/TESTING.md at main · jcostacs/ai-units-quota-workflow","appPayload":{},"meta":{"title":"ai-units-quota-workflow/TESTING.md at main · jcostacs/ai-units-quota-workflow"}}</script>

<p dir="auto"><strong>Workflow:</strong> AI Units Quota Check<br>
<strong>Test date:</strong> 2026-09-23<br>
<strong>Environment:</strong> Internal sprint/hardening environment (non-production SaaS)</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Summary</h2><a id="user-content-summary" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Summary" href="#summary"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">End-to-end validation of the workflow was completed successfully in a hardening environment. All core enforcement tasks passed. Several environment-specific issues were encountered and resolved — documented below so production deployment goes smoothly.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Test Approach</h2><a id="user-content-test-approach" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Test Approach" href="#test-approach"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The hardening environment used for enforcement testing did not have AI Units billing data, so the DQL task was patched to inject a test user directly and the lockout JavaScript was modified to hardcode an email address. This allowed full validation of the enforcement chain. Real billing event schema was subsequently validated against an internal dev tenant — see §Billing Event Schema Validation below.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Temporary changes made for testing (do not carry into production):</strong></p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Task</th>
<th>Temporary change</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>check_for_ai_quota</code></td>
<td>Replaced real DQL with <code>filter false</code> + hardcoded test email inject</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>lock_out_users</code></td>
<td>Replaced credential vault lookup with hardcoded <code>accountId</code> and OAuth secret</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>lock_out_users</code></td>
<td>Removed predecessor condition to allow unconditional execution</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto">All changes were reverted before committing the production <code>workflow.yaml</code>.</p>
<hr>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Issues Encountered &amp; Resolutions</h2><a id="user-content-issues-encountered--resolutions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Issues Encountered &amp; Resolutions" href="#issues-encountered--resolutions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. DQL does not support block comments (<code>/* ... */</code>)</h3><a id="user-content-1-dql-does-not-support-block-comments---" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. DQL does not support block comments (/* ... */)" href="#1-dql-does-not-support-block-comments---"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Symptom:</strong> <code>check_for_ai_quota</code> failed with <code>PARSE_ERROR: ':' isn't allowed here</code>.<br>
<strong>Cause:</strong> The DQL query contained a <code>/* ... */</code> block comment, which the Grail parser does not support.<br>
<strong>Fix:</strong> Removed all block comments from the DQL. Single-line <code>//</code> comments within pipeline steps are supported.<br>
<strong>Production impact:</strong> The production <code>workflow.yaml</code> in this repo has no block comments — this is already fixed.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Hardening environment requires different OAuth and API base URLs</h3><a id="user-content-2-hardening-environment-requires-different-oauth-and-api-base-urls" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Hardening environment requires different OAuth and API base URLs" href="#2-hardening-environment-requires-different-oauth-and-api-base-urls"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Symptom:</strong> OAuth token request returned <code>400 invalid_request</code> with empty error description.<br>
<strong>Cause:</strong> The workflow uses <code>sso.dynatrace.com</code> and <code>api.dynatrace.com</code> (production SaaS defaults), which are unreachable from the hardening environment.<br>
<strong>Fix:</strong> Swapped <code>tokenUrl</code> and <code>baseUrl</code> to the internal environment equivalents of the SSO and Account Management API endpoints.<br>
<strong>Production impact:</strong> None. The production <code>workflow.yaml</code> uses <code>sso.dynatrace.com</code> / <code>api.dynatrace.com</code>, which are correct for customer SaaS environments.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Outbound hosts must be in the AppEngine allowlist</h3><a id="user-content-3-outbound-hosts-must-be-in-the-appengine-allowlist" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Outbound hosts must be in the AppEngine allowlist" href="#3-outbound-hosts-must-be-in-the-appengine-allowlist"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Symptom:</strong> After correcting the URLs, requests were blocked with <code>NotCapable: Blocked request to '...' (host not in allowlist)</code>.<br>
<strong>Cause:</strong> AppEngine sandboxes outbound connections to an explicit allowlist.<br>
<strong>Fix:</strong> The hardening environment already had the sprint/hardening SSO and API hosts in its allowlist. No action was needed beyond selecting the correct URLs.<br>
<strong>Production impact:</strong> For production customer environments, <code>sso.dynatrace.com</code> and <code>api.dynatrace.com</code> should already be in the default allowlist. If a customer hits this error, they need to add those hosts via <strong>Settings → Preferences → Allowlist for outbound connections</strong>.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Credential vault <code>getCredentialsDetails</code> returned 403 in hardening</h3><a id="user-content-4-credential-vault-getcredentialsdetails-returned-403-in-hardening" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Credential vault getCredentialsDetails returned 403 in hardening" href="#4-credential-vault-getcredentialsdetails-returned-403-in-hardening"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Symptom:</strong> <code>credentialVaultClient.getCredentialsDetails</code> returned <code>403: The requested credential details cannot be accessed by the current user or entity</code> even with correct AppEngine scope and access settings.<br>
<strong>Cause:</strong> Not fully diagnosed — likely a hardening-environment-specific restriction on credential access for AppEngine workflows. The actor authorization settings, credential scope, and access were all correctly configured.<br>
<strong>Workaround:</strong> Credentials were hardcoded directly in the script for testing purposes and removed before committing.<br>
<strong>Production impact:</strong> This 403 was not reproduced in any production SaaS environment. The production <code>workflow.yaml</code> uses the standard <code>getCredentialsDetails</code> approach (identical to the original <a href="https://github.com/Dynatrace/community-examples/tree/main/cost-intelligence-blueprints/set-quotas-with-workflow">Log Query Quota Workflow</a> which works in production). Monitor this on first production deployment and escalate if the 403 reappears.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5. Workflow actor authorization settings required</h3><a id="user-content-5-workflow-actor-authorization-settings-required" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5. Workflow actor authorization settings required" href="#5-workflow-actor-authorization-settings-required"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Symptom:</strong> First execution failed with <code>Could not run workflow task on behalf of '&lt;uuid&gt;'. Please ensure Authorization Settings are configured</code>.<br>
<strong>Cause:</strong> The workflow Actor (service user) had not been configured in <strong>Settings → Automations → Authorization settings</strong>.<br>
<strong>Fix:</strong> Changed the Actor to the deploying user, who already had full authorization settings configured.<br>
<strong>Production impact:</strong> Documented in the README setup steps. The Actor must have Authorization Settings configured before the workflow can execute.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Production Deployment Checklist</h2><a id="user-content-production-deployment-checklist" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Production Deployment Checklist" href="#production-deployment-checklist"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For the colleague deploying to production, verify the following before publishing:</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> <code>event.type == "AI Units"</code> confirmed against production billing events (<code>fetch dt.system.events | filter event.kind == "BILLING_USAGE_EVENT" | dedup event.type</code>)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Metric field <code>usage.quantity.billable</code> confirmed against a real AI Units billing event (see §Billing Event Schema Validation below)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Quota threshold (default: 1,000) reviewed and adjusted for the target customer</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> IAM deny policy created and attached to "AI Quota Exceeded" group (consult Dynatrace IAM docs for the appropriate permission to deny)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> OAuth client created with an Account Manager user as subject, scopes: <code>account-idm-read</code>, <code>account-idm-write</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Credential vault entry <code>Quota check OAuth</code> created (Username = Account UUID, Password = full OAuth client secret, Scope = AppEngine)</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> Workflow Actor has Authorization Settings configured</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> <code>sso.dynatrace.com</code> and <code>api.dynatrace.com</code> are in the environment's outbound allowlist</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> <code>send_email_about_ai_quota</code> tested with a real email recipient before go-live</li>
<li class="task-list-item"><input type="checkbox" id="" disabled="" class="task-list-item-checkbox" aria-label="Incomplete task"> <code>quota_reset_at_midnight</code> validated after first production run at 00:00 UTC</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Billing Event Schema Validation</h2><a id="user-content-billing-event-schema-validation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Billing Event Schema Validation" href="#billing-event-schema-validation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">A real <code>AI Units</code> billing event was observed in an internal Dynatrace environment on 2026-09-23. The event confirmed the following field schema:</p>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Field</th>
<th>Value observed</th>
<th>Workflow uses</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>event.kind</code></td>
<td><code>BILLING_USAGE_EVENT</code></td>
<td><code>filter event.kind == "BILLING_USAGE_EVENT"</code> ✓</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>event.type</code></td>
<td><code>AI Units</code></td>
<td><code>filter event.type == "AI Units"</code> ✓</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>usage.quantity.billable</code></td>
<td><code>30.00</code> (double)</td>
<td><code>sum(usage.quantity.billable)</code> ✓</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>usage.unit</code></td>
<td><code>Units</code></td>
<td>— (informational)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>user.email</code></td>
<td>present (e.g. <code>user@dynatrace.com</code>)</td>
<td><code>by:{user.email}</code> / <code>fields email = user.email</code> ✓</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>event.id</code></td>
<td>UUID</td>
<td><code>dedup event.id</code> ✓</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>event.version</code></td>
<td><code>1.0.0</code></td>
<td><em>(filter removed — correct, "1.0.0" ≠ "1.0")</em> ✓</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>event.provider</code></td>
<td><code>LIMA_USAGE_STREAM</code></td>
<td>— (not filtered)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>dt.openpipeline.source</code></td>
<td><code>system_events</code></td>
<td>fetched via <code>fetch dt.system.events</code> ✓</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Key findings:</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>usage.quantity.billable</code> is confirmed as the correct metric field. The field <code>consumed_units</code> does not exist in real events.</li>
<li><code>event.version</code> is <code>"1.0.0"</code>, not <code>"1.0"</code>. Removing the <code>event.version == "1.0"</code> filter (present in the original log quota blueprint) was correct — keeping it would have caused the query to silently return no results.</li>
<li><code>user.email</code> is always present (1,463/1,463 events checked had a non-null value). Per-user quota enforcement is safe to rely on.</li>
<li><code>usage.start</code> and <code>usage.end</code> are both null in practice. The event <code>timestamp</code> is the usage time, confirming <code>from: -0d@d</code> as the correct daily window. <strong>Reviewer note:</strong> <code>-0d@d</code> is DQL's calendar-day anchor — the <code>@d</code> suffix snaps to midnight UTC at the start of today, making it a calendar-day window (00:00 UTC → now), not a rolling <code>now()-24h</code>. This is intentional and aligns with the midnight reset in <code>quota_reset_at_midnight</code>.</li>
<li><code>caller.type</code> takes three observed values: <code>internal</code> (workflow/operator actions — the dominant type), <code>api</code> (direct API calls), and <code>mcp</code> (MCP tool calls). All share <code>tool.category: ai</code>. The workflow counts all caller types toward the quota. Customers who want to restrict the quota to specific interaction types (e.g. only <code>api</code> calls) can add <code>| filter caller.type == "api"</code> to both DQL tasks.</li>
<li>Service accounts appear with a UUID-based email format (<code>&lt;uuid&gt;@service.sso.dynatrace.com</code>). The quota applies to these identities as well. Customers who want to exempt service accounts can add <code>| filterOut matchesPhrase(user.email, "@service.sso.dynatrace.com")</code> to both DQL tasks.</li>
<li>Real-world consumption over 7 days in an active internal tenant ranged from ~51 to ~38,250 units per user. Only one user exceeded 1,000 units in a single day, validating that 1,000 units/day is a reasonable default threshold for most customer environments..</li>
</ul>
</article></div><div class="d-none"></div></section></div></div></div> </div> <!-- --> </div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="ScrollMarksContainer-module__scrollMarksContainer__Eu7uU" id="find-result-marks-container"></div><div class="d-none"></div><div class="d-none"></div></div> <!-- --> <!-- --> </div>
</react-app>




  </div>

</turbo-frame>

    </main>
  </div>
